每份 ~280 行, 总 ~1351 行 ~80K text. 5 sections per report (Top papers / BibTeX / Synthesis / Counter-evidence / Forward citation chain) per unified template。

**关键 finding (按 paper-impact 排序)**:

#### ⭐⭐⭐ Q3 — Paper §1 first-work claim VERIFIED

> "**A pronounced methodological gap: no study isolates SoM-style flat text as a standalone observation without its accompanying marked screenshot. The target paper fills this unprecedented gap.**"

→ Phantom-SoM paper §1 hook "first systematic SoM-text isolation" claim **lit-verified novel**. Reviewer attack vector "你不是 first" 已 preempted via Q3 forward citation chain analysis (FOCUSAGENT prunes hierarchical AXTree but doesn't compare to flat list; HMT compares formats in memory architecture not observation; Yang 2023 SoM bundles text with image).

这是 today's biggest paper §1 hook win, paper acceptance probability ↑↑。

#### ⭐⭐⭐ Q1 — Mirage 70-80% claim cross-validated + counter-evidence catalog

**Quantitative anchors confirmed**:
- Asadi 2026 Mirage 70-80% ✅ (cross-VLM-family: GPT-4V/4o, Claude 3, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Qwen-VL, LLaVA, InternVL)
- Kaduri 2024 96% accuracy with 5% image tokens
- VWA empirical: text-only 7.25% / multimodal 16.37% = **44% relative retention** (more conservative than 70-80% on real web)
- Plan-and-Act 2025: **81.36% text-only on WebVoyager** (text-only SOTA)
- XLRS-Bench: text-only Qwen3-8B 51.6% > multimodal GPT-4o 45.2% (text-only **beats** multimodal!)

**Counter-evidence (paper §1 honest framing必要)**:
- AsgardBench 2026 / DailyDroid 2026 / Mind2Web Deep Research / FileGramOS — text-only collapses on perception-conditioned, dynamic GUI, behavioral tracking tasks
- → Paper §1 hook 应 explicit qualify: "text-only fallback works on standard web schema with stable DOM; fails on perception-conditioned tasks"

#### ⭐⭐ Q4 — Scaffold Effect "no fundamental counter-evidence"

> "no fundamental counter-evidence directly contradicting the core premise of the Scaffold Effect"

→ Paper §5 axis 2 Mirage subspace 论证 robustly anchored。Vu & Balloccu 2026 临床 finding 跨 domain hold。Counter-evidence 仅 edge-case (visual-first adapters / Omni-Modal Dissonance / CEBaG Bayesian detection)。

#### ⭐⭐ Q6 — Lazy Minimization confirmed with caveats

- Scaling Law of Redundancy: large VLMs robust to image pruning, small VLMs collapse
- Pre-training mixture: Qwen2-VL-7B text-only fine-tune slightly outperforms image-text (50.50% vs 50.00%) for complex conceptual learning
- Counter-evidence: ChartGemma 3B beats GPT-4o on chart QA (specialized fine-tune); InternVL3 8B = Gemini 2.5 Pro under specific markers; See&Trek shows perf drops on Relative Direction / Object Counting

→ Paper §7 prose framing: "Lazy Minimization holds for **standard generalist VLMs**, with domain-specialized fine-tuned small models as exceptions"。

#### ⚠️ Q2 — surprise finding tension with paper §2 axis 2 framing

> "Text-based DOM-element referencing consistently outperforms Set-of-Mark visual bounding boxes for prompt referencing due to better alignment with textual pre-training"

→ Paper §2 axis 2 framing 现"SoM-prompt activates visual-mark referencing mental model" 隐含 SoM-prompt 是 advantageous. Lit Q2 suggests DOM-element-id > SoM [N] markers at prompt referencing level.

**Resolution**: 区分 "prompt referencing system" (DOM element_id vs SoM [N]) 跟 "obs format" (AXTree vs [SOM_MARKS] flat list). Paper §2 P-text 是 obs-format swap (axis 1) 用 DOM-prompt + [SOM_MARKS] obs, 跟 Q2 finding 不直接 contradict 但 paper §2 prose 必须 careful 区分 这两个 dimension。

Other Q2 counter-evidence:
- Zheng 2024 EMNLP 162 personas 实证 NO effect on accuracy
- System wrapper can hurt up to 10pp on Llama 3 when conflicts with user task

→ Paper §2/§5 prose 应 honest "prompt format effects are conditional, not universally beneficial"。

### 108.18 Q5 Bidirectional modality fusion returned (2026-05-01 evening, 6/6 complete)

**触发**: Q5 (`Vision-Language Model Modality Interaction A Comprehensive Analysis of Bidirectional Dominance and Failure Modes.md`) ~221 行 ~37KB 终于 return (Gemini quota unlock). 这是 paper §2 axis 3 (image axis 8-channel framework) 的 lit anchor 厚度回应 — 之前 axis 3 是 paper §2 三 axis 中 lit anchor 最薄的一支 (单点 Tong 2024 / Li POPE / Bitton-Guetta), 现在拿到 cross-benchmark 综合。

**Q5 核心贡献**:

1. **Bidirectional Failure framing 是 "novel synthesis"** (Q5 doc line 22 自标): "framing VLM modality interaction as exhibiting **dual failure modes that act in opposite directions** constitutes a novel theoretical synthesis. Current 2023-2026 literature largely treats these failure modes in isolation". → Paper §5 axis 3 prose 可 cite Q5 综述 + claim "first systematic web-agent multi-step application" — layered novelty (general framework Q5 综述层面 novel, web-agent 应用层 paper first).

2. **Dual-axis same-paper benchmarks** (paper bidirectional framing 不是 artifact 的硬证):
   - **Guan 2024 HallusionBench** (CVPR, arXiv:2310.14566): GPT-4V 31.42% / 其他开源 <16% on dual-axis control pairs; "visual illusion" + "language hallucination" 同 suite 测
   - **Sun 2023 MMHal-Bench**: 8 distinct error categories, both axes simultaneously

3. **Per-axis benchmark mapping**:
   - Image-over-text (M1 saliency hijack): Li 2023 POPE / CHAIR
   - Text-over-vision (M2 language prior override): Tong 2024 / Fu 2024 BLINK 24-30% near random / Bitton-Guetta 2023 WHOOPS! / MM-Vet OCR fallback

4. **Scaling law on bidirectional bias** (paper §7 cross-capability anchor):
   - Large LLM (70B+) + compressed vision → text-over-vision (vision sink, cross-attention decay)
   - Small LLM (7B) + uncompressed dense vision → image-over-text (dense tokens overwhelm small LLM)
   - 这给 B0 (235B-A22B MoE) vs B1 (4B) 不对称预测: B1 更倾向 image-over-text dominance, B0 long-trajectory 更倾向 text-over-vision drift — 可作 paper §7 mechanism prediction validation

5. **SoM-specific harming channels formalized** (paper §5 axis 3 8-channel cross-paper validation):
   - SoM occlusion → image-over-text (saliency hijack), markers as "embedded geometric shape" distractor (跟 §100 -60pp OCR 一致)
   - Numeric attention hijack at high mark density → text-over-vision (prior override), LLM "latches onto numeric IDs as primary reasoning anchors" (跟 §100 num_ids 0→446 一致)
   - 这两条 SoM channels dominance direction **opposite** — 正是我们 axis 3 8-channel "bidirectional fusion" framework 的 lit anchor

6. **Mid-layer attention decay** (Liu 2025 "Devils in Middle Layers" arXiv:2512.07730): object hallucination 是 multi-faceted, 同时由 mid-layer visual attention decay + decoding-time language prior 导致。**Paper §5 axis 3 dual-cause anchor** (从单 cause 升级到 dual-cause).

**Counter-evidence**:
- "Seeing but Not Believing" 2025: linear probing 显示 visual encoder 准确提取 features, failure 是 **late-stage generative disconnect** 而非 cross-modal failure. → Paper §5 prose 不应 over-commit encoding-stage attribution; 我们 8-channel 是 behavioral-level taxonomy
- M3amba 2025: BiMamba blocks 在 pathology specialized domain achieve lossless integration → bidirectional dominance 不是架构必然, 是 generalist VLM typical pattern (paper §7 specialized exception caveat)
- MM-Vet: failure mode "task-dependent / both directions" — single dominance direction framing 可能 oversimplify; 与 paper "site-modulated" framing aligned

**Integration 落地 (this commit, 2026-05-01 evening)**:

- ✅ `paper_planning.md` §2 Zoom 3 expansion: 加 "Q5 Bidirectional modality fusion" subsection (~30 行)
- ✅ `paper_planning.md` §2 Zoom 3 counter-evidence catalog: 加第 4 组 "M3 (Image axis bidirectional dominance) counter-anchors"
- ✅ `paper_planning.md` §2 Axis 3 (line 372+): 顶部加 "Cross-paper lit anchor stack (Q5)" 段落 + 8-channel 各 channel 补 Q5 lit cite (3e/3f/3h/3i)
- ✅ 笔记 §108.18 (this entry): chronicle Q5 returned + integration

## 109. Dual-track reframe + 9-cell taxonomy + 3-round DR (2026-05-04 evening) [design][literature][infra] #design #literature #infra

**触发**: 学长 5/3 16:05 message "我们也可以考虑目前网页可以怎么更加优化去符合 agent 使用习惯。不光光从 agent 本身出发，环境也是可以的。两头出发。然后也可以变相证明 agent 本身的 routing 也是可泛化的"。我先误解为"加新方向", 5/4 evening 用户 push back + 给出实证 context (跟 Avenir Web 团队聊过 / VWA select dropdown 7 层 fix / viewport bug / scroll bar 缺失 等), 重新整理后发现学长其实是讲**显性 frame 我们已经在做的事**。

### 109.1 关键 insight: 我们一直在做 dual-track 但没 explicit frame

**用户 5/4 evening 给的 context** (这个解读改变全 paper landscape 定位):
- VWA bug + Avenir Web fix 都是"环境不适配 agent"的体现 (web 设计初衷给人看, 不是给 agent 看)
- Avenir Web 团队**直接 ignore cookie banner / popup 等环境侧 issues**, 不算 failure
- 我们做的 9 条 environmental fix (§51-62 select dropdown 7 层 / §53 confirm 弹窗 / §80 viewport ratio / §81 Wikipedia ZIM / §103 Magento auth / §107 Phase A 4-cluster) 全是 environment intervention work
- SoM 在 conceptual 层面就是 environment-affordance synthesis (虽然 implementation 是 agent-pipeline-side script)
- 用户原选 VWA 不选 online mind2web 是因为 routing 需要 controlled environment — paper 1 必须 controlled, paper 2 才 generalize 到 live web

**学长 push 的真正意义**: 不是 "加 environment-side 新方向", 是 **"把已做 work 升级成 explicit framework"**。

### 109.2 9-cell intervention taxonomy 创建

整夜 audit 笔记 §1-§108, ~40 entries 进 9-cell matrix:

```
                (i) Bug fix    (ii) Synthesis    (iii) Channel addition
L1 Server-side    ~6 ✅           NLWeb (deployed)    NLWeb / agent.txt RFC
L2 Pipeline       ~28 ✅          OmniParser-v2       (gap)
L3 LLM-internal   n/a            ⭐ Phantom routing   n/a
                                 (paper-1 niche)
```

(ii)×L3 内部还分 4 个 sub-tier (paper §1 hook 关键 differentiator):
- Pretraining-time: Magma (Microsoft, 用 Qwen3-VL backbone)
- Fine-tuning-time: ScribeAgent (CMU, fine-tune Qwen 7B 6B token corpus)
- RAG-offline-explore: AppAgent-v2 (Tencent)
- **Inference-time only: 我们 paper** ← 没 named industry 占这格

落地: paper_planning §21 (新建, ~190 行) 5 个 subsection. 笔记 §1-§108 ~40 条 § 系统映射进 9 cells, 跟 paper findings (§3/§4/§9/§20/§23/§27/§49/§56/§77/§89/§103/§104/§106/§108) 严格分开。

**关键发现 — (iii)×L2 self-perception channel-addition 7 条 § 共享 same root cause**:
- §52/§64 type 全选变蓝 (scroll 后 input 失焦 agent 不知)
- §55 delete 成功信号缺失 (笔记 explicit "暂不修复, 非结构性缺失")
- §72 Qwen scroll_up 3-7% (agent 不知 page-Y position)
- §72 B1 scroll 到底不停 (agent 不知 page bottom)
- §31 task 19/22/58 auto-scroll 不可预测
- §32 Vision 坐标 misclick 零自纠正
- §96 B1 32% click 非交互元素

→ paper §5 mechanism shared ceiling argument 直接由这 7 条撑: "phantom routing space 4-fold drop-in 是测在固定 (iii)×L2 channel-absent ceiling 下的 (ii)×L3 substitution capability"。

### 109.3 别扭 framework + post-hoc 区分 (跟 5/3 reframe 一致)

5/3 用户 push 把 paper hook 从"3-arm a-priori commit"改成"hero P-SoM + structural ablation + framing rule R1-R5", 笔记 §108.16 别扭 framework + capability-modulated reversal 全标 post-hoc。5/4 整理时进一步 explicit:
- (A) Hero deployment (P-SoM, gating strict)
- (B) Structural claim (P-text/P-prompt non-overlap, low-threshold)
- (C) Post-hoc (别扭 / capability reversal) — paper §5/§7 写明
- (D) Framing rule R1-R5 — data-conditional

### 109.4 Sweet Spot env-side pilot 设计 (用户 push back catch tautology trap)

**用户原话**: "server 自己吐出来 select 菜单里面隐藏的字段"

**Tautology trap (用户 catch)**: 如果 server 端 emit byte-equivalent [SOM_MARKS], 就是 implementation relocation 不是 substrate change。我之前提的 "Sweet Spot B HTTP header" 落入这个陷阱, 撤回。

**真正 sweet spot**: server emit **agent script 看不到的信息** (closed dropdown options / 视口外 elements / backend semantic / page-state metadata)。如果 agent 用 server-emitted JSON 复现 routing benefit → routing 不绑死在"agent 自己提取" substrate, 是 problem geometry property → 学长说的"变相证明 agent routing 可泛化" 成立。

工程实现 (1 site pilot, ~3-5 d):
- Postmill (classifieds) Symfony middleware emit `/ask` endpoint
- 内容: schema.org JSON 含 elements + select hidden options + page state metadata
- Agent 加 mode `phantom_server` 读 endpoint, 跟 phantom_text 对比

### 109.5 3 轮 Gemini Deep Research

**Round-1** (`docs/literature/Cost-Aware Routing for Vision-Language Web Agents...md` 不是这次, 是历史 Q1) — 不 cover 中文生态, 不 cover OmniParser/Magma/NLWeb, Section E 用我们术语 circular framing。User push back: "你是 claude, 应该知道现在 frontier 强到什么程度, 豆包手机 / openclaw 等等"。

**Round-2** (`docs/literature/Dual-Track Agent and Environment Routing A Comprehensive Analysis of State-of-the-Art Computer-Use Systems.md`, 5/3 fired): 296 行, 加 Avenir-Web / Anthropic Claude Computer Use / OpenAI Operator+CUA / Codex CLI / Google Mariner / academic SOTA。但漏中文 + Section E circular。User push: "感觉很多都不够新, 不够深"。

**Round-2 second file** (`docs/literature/State of Agent-Environment Co-Design in Computer-Use Systems.md`, 5/4 fired with refined prompt): 256 行, 中文生态全 cover (UI-TARS / Doubao Mobile / Manus/OpenManus / AutoGLM-Web / AppAgent-v2 / CogAgent / OS-Atlas-Genesis), 加 OmniParser-v2 / Magma / ShowUI / AGUVIS / Atlas / Browser-Use SDK, NLWeb May 2025 confirm。Section E 独立 framing (不用 phantom routing 术语)。但**6 处 hallucination**: HMT author 编 "Huang et al." / NLAH 凑名字 / K3 Mariner 编造 / WebAIM 数字错 / Operator 41.7% misattribute / Doubao 商业封号无 verified。

**Round-3** (`docs/literature/Agent-Environment Integration and Co-Design Architectural Realities, Security Profiles, and Economic Benchmarks.md`, 5/4 evening): 246 行 fact-check 全部 Round-2 hallucinations, 加 implementation deep-dives (OmniParser-v2 SPS literal format / Magma SoM+ToM 训练机制 + **Qwen3-VL backbone**! / AppAgent-v2 view_state_id JSON schema / ScribeAgent **Qwen 7B fine-tune** WebArena 51.3% / UI-TARS System-2 CoT) + 新 angles (game-like envs Cradle/GenSim, security IDPI taxonomy + MCP 100% spoofing + Sonnet 4.6 IDPI resistance, cost/latency anchors 241K vs 47-140K tokens, Mind2Web 2 WebJudge 3-category taxonomy + WebGym infrastructure, NLWeb 实际 deployed at Tripadvisor + Shopify with `/ask`+`/mcp` schema.org JSON spec)。

**6 处 fact-check 修正** (Round-3 verified):
| Round-2 错的 | Round-3 修正 |
|---|---|
| HMT by "Huang et al." | by Tan/Gao/Wu (BIT), arXiv:2603.07024 ✅ |
| NLAH AGENTS.md by Hashimoto | Hallucinated 凑名字 — 实际 arXiv:2602.11327 是 Anbiaee et al. 安全论文, NLAH 是 arXiv:2603.25723 不同 paper |
| K3 Mariner / ActionEngine | 完全 hallucinated, 不存在 |
| WebAIM 2026 "57 vs 27" | 实际 59.1 vs 42 (2026); 27 是 YoY ARIA growth % |
| Operator 41.7% failure rate | Misattribution: 实际是 MAI-UI 41.7% **success** on MobileWorld |
| Doubao Mobile 商业封号 | 无 Chinese press verification, 删除 |

### 109.6 关键 paper-shifting discovery: Magma + ScribeAgent 都用 Qwen base

**Round-3 deep-dive 发现**:
- Magma 用 **Qwen3-VL backbone**, integrate SoM + ToM 进 weights via pretraining
- ScribeAgent 用 **Qwen 7B base**, fine-tune 6B token DOM workflow corpus, WebArena 45.7→51.3%
- 我们 paper B0 = Qwen3-VL-235B / B1 = Qwen3-VL-4B, **同 family**

→ Paper §1 hook 升级 differentiator: "Magma integrates SoM + ToM into Qwen3-VL pretraining; ScribeAgent fine-tunes Qwen on 6B token DOM corpus to WebArena 51.3%; we use **non-pretrained, non-fine-tuned Qwen3-VL** at inference time. Phantom routing space isolates the contribution of inference-time prompt structure from pretraining/fine-tuning contributions, requiring no model retraining." — clean experimental isolation, reviewer 一眼 buy。

### 109.7 NLWeb 是 deployed 不是 proposal (Round-3 verified)

> "NLWeb is not a theoretical proposal; it is actively deployed. Early adopters include major commercial entities like Tripadvisor and Shopify."

NLWeb 双 endpoint:
- `/ask` — conversational queries → schema.org JSON
- `/mcp` — MCP protocol integration

literal JSON spec 例:

```json
{
  "@context": "https://schema.org",

  "mcp_actionable": {"endpoint": "/mcp/cart/add", "parameters": ["sku", "quantity"]}
}
```

→ 我们 Sweet Spot env-side pilot **直接 mirror NLWeb spec** in VWA Postmill/Magento, 是首个 controlled comparison of NLWeb-style emission vs L3 inference substitution。

### 109.10 教训 + 流程改进

**教训 1**: LLM-generated lit review 不能直接 trust。Round-2 6 处 hallucination 全是 confident 拼凑名字 (Hashimoto / Huang / 错数字), 直觉看不出来。**Paper writing 前必须 verify 每一条 industry citation**。

**教训 2**: DR prompt 设计影响 output 质量极大。Round-1 prompt 太宽泛 → 漏中文。Round-2 prompt 加 explicit "list these specific systems" → cover 全。Round-3 prompt 加"fact-check + implementation deep-dive + missing angles 3 阶段" → output 直接 paper-ready。

**教训 3**: 分文件存 DR (`docs/literature/<title>.md`) 比单文件 append 好 — 每轮独立 + 引用方便。

**教训 4**: User push back 是 paper rigor 的关键。Sweet Spot B (HTTP header) 我提了, 用户 catch tautology trap。如果直接做下去就是浪费时间设计 deceptive cross-validation。**讨论时多 push 假设**, 比 1 人 think tank 好得多。

### 109.13 Token economy filter-scope distinction discovery — paper §3 method rigor signal

**用户 sharp observation**: agent-browser 200-400 tokens vs full DOM 3000-5000 = 10-15× compression; 但我们 paper [SOM_MARKS] ~3000 tokens (§1 table 实测 cls 2948-3008 / red 3437-3661) 跟 DOM AXTree 几乎一样大 (±7%). **为什么 industry SDK 这么压缩, 我们没?**

**Root cause analysis** (paper §3 method 必 disclose):
- Industry SDK (Playwright MCP / agent-browser / Tarsier / Stagehand): 仅 emit **interactive elements** (button/link/input/select), 跳过 StaticText/heading/image — 估计 70-80% reduction 来自此
- 我们 paper: 按 §96 design 决定 **保留全 elements** — 因为 DOM↔SoM ablation 必须只 vary format 不 vary info density (§96 笔记 explicit 论证: "保留全部元素 for **路由研究控制变量** + **Phantom-SoM 消融前提**")

**Two orthogonal compression axes** (paper-strategic upgrade):
1. **Substitution mechanism** (visual marker render → textual ref list): 我们 paper 单独 isolate 这个 axis, characterize phantom routing space
2. **Interactive-only filtering**: industry deployment optimization, **stackable on top** of substitution conclusions

**Paper rigor 加分**: 
- 我们 paper prove substitution 在 **3000+ token full-information density** 下成立 — 比 industry interactive-only-filtered 200-400 token deployment 更 stringent test
- Practitioners 拿 paper conclusion + interactive-only filter = industry SDK style 部署
- Reviewer attack "你 [SOM_MARKS] ~3000 tokens 比 industry 200-400 大很多, 为什么不 filter" → response "filter 跟 substitution 是 orthogonal axis, paper 单独 isolate substitution; filter 是 paper-1 conclusion 之上的 deployment 进一步优化"

**Paper §3 method 候选 prose** (落地 paper writing 时):
> "P79's [SOM_MARKS] preserves ALL accessibility-tree elements (interactive AND
> non-interactive: StaticText, heading, image, etc.) per §96 design rationale,
> in contrast to industry SDK practice (Playwright MCP, agent-browser, Tarsier,
> Stagehand) of filtering to interactive-only elements. This higher information
> density (~3000 tokens vs industry ~200-400) maintains experimental control:
> clean DOM↔SoM ablation requires varying only format (tree-text vs flat-list),
> not information density. Filter pruning would confound format and density
> dimensions, breaking Phantom-SoM ablation prerequisite (§25/§96). Industry
> interactive-only filtering can be applied on top of phantom routing space
> conclusions for further deployment-grade compression — substitution axis
> (paper main characterization) and filtering axis (industry optimization) are
> orthogonal."

### 109.14 Playwright vs CDP substrate clarification

**Update mental model**:
- **CDP (Chrome DevTools Protocol)** 是 lowest-level common substrate
- **Playwright** 是 CDP wrapper (Python/JS, + Firefox/WebKit support)
- **CDP-direct tools** (agent-browser Rust, OpenClaw) skip Playwright for performance

**Engine choice 是 implementation detail, 不影响 paper claim about routing structure**:
- 我们 paper 用 Playwright via VWA — engineering choice for evaluation harness
- agent-browser 用 CDP-direct Rust — engineering choice for production
- 两者都坐 (ii)×L2 cell, 不同 implementation 路径

**Paper §3 method 一句话 acknowledge**: "We use Playwright via VWA's vwa_wrapper.py harness; CDP-direct (agent-browser) is engineering optimization orthogonal to substitution-gradient claim."

### 109.16 Fact-check correction: scope vs format axis (2026-05-04 deepest evening)

**触发**: 用户 push back — "你看我们的 `results/visualwebarena/phase1/B1_phantom_prompt_classifieds_20260501/.../step_000/observation_dom.txt`, 也是清洗过后的啊"。我之前 §109.13 写"industry SDK interactive-only filter / P79 preserve all elements" — 用户 catch 这是 over-claim, 因为我们 observation_dom.txt 实际也是 cleaned 过的 a11y tree text。

**Fact-check 步骤** (今天教训: 写 paper-strategic claim 前先读代码):
1. 读 `results/.../classifieds_task_0/step_000/observation_dom.txt` 实际内容: 仅 32 行, 含 link / button / heading / StaticText / image / textbox / combobox / RootWebArea — **全是 a11y-roled elements, 没有 raw `<div>` `<span>` wrapper**
2. 读 `external/visualwebarena/browser_env/processors.py:513-619` 找 extraction logic
3. 验证: `parse_accessibility_tree` (line 513-597) + `clean_accesibility_tree` (line 599-619) 是 3 层 filter:
   - Layer A: Chrome accessibility tree 内部 已过滤 purely presentational
   - Layer B: role-based empty-node drop — `if not name.strip() and not properties and role in ["generic", "img", "list", "strong", "paragraph", "banner", "navigation", "Section", "LabelText", "Legend", "listitem"]: valid_node = False` (line 553-568)
   - Layer C: StaticText dedup — "remove statictext if the content already appears in the previous 3 lines" (line 599-619)
4. 验证: URL property 来自 Chrome a11y tree 标准 property (`IGNORED_ACTREE_PROPERTIES = ("focusable", "editable", "readonly", "level", "settable", ...)` constants.py:288, **url 不在 ignore list**), 不是 VWA 加的 — industry SDK 也有这个 property 但 trim 掉 (agent-browser README example `- heading 'Example Domain' [ref=e1]` 不含 url)

**修正**:

| 之前 (错的) | 实际 (verified by code) |
|---|---|
| "industry SDK interactive-only filter, P79 preserve all elements" | Both use a11y-tree-roled scope; gap is **format-axis** not scope-axis |
| "10-15× compression source = filter scope" | 10-15× 是 industry SDK 跟 raw HTML 比, 不是跟 a11y-roled 比. Snapshot-alone gap 是 ~3-5× from format trim |
| "P79 ~3000 tokens snapshot" | 3000+ tokens 是 **full prompt context** (system + task + observation + history); observation snapshot alone 约 ~1000-1500 tokens |
| "Filter-scope orthogonal axis" | **Format-axis orthogonal** — URL/property/hierarchy/ref width emission, not filter scope |
| "§96 design preserves all elements vs industry filter" | §96 比较 P79 SoM marker scope vs **VWA-original ImageObservationProcessor** (interactive-only screenshot annotation), 都 within a11y-roled scope. Industry SDK 也 a11y-roled. |

**正确 framing** (paper §3 method 用):

> "Industry SDK and P79 paper share similar element scope (a11y-tree-roled
> elements via Chrome accessibility tree extraction; both filter purely
> presentational HTML wrappers). Token-economy gap is format-axis: P79
> emits Chrome a11y standard URL property, additional a11y properties
> (`focused: True` / `required: False` / `expanded: False`), tab-indented
> hierarchy, longer ref format (`[id=88]` vs `@e88`); industry SDK trims
> compact format. Snapshot-alone token estimate: P79 ~1000-1500 vs
> industry ~200-400 = ~3-5× format-axis gap. The §1 hook table 3008-3661
> tokens refers to full prompt context (system + task + observation +
> history), not observation alone. Format-axis trims (industry deployment
> optimization) and substitution-axis (paper main characterization) are
> orthogonal — practitioners can stack format trims on phantom routing
> conclusions for deployment compression."

**教训 (process improvement)**:
1. **写 paper-strategic claim 前必须读代码** — 我 4 turn 之前都没读过 processors.py, 直接接受 "VWA preserve all DOM" 的 wrong intuition
2. **User push-back 比 self-audit 强** — 用户 sharp question "你确定?" 直接 catch 了 4 turn 错误 framing
3. **Industry SDK README 例子要细读** — agent-browser example `- heading 'Example Domain' [ref=e1]` 含 heading 不是 interactive-only, 我之前 skim 没注意
4. **§96 design rationale 准确含义需 careful 读** — "保留全部元素" 比较的是 P79 vs VWA 原版 SoM marker, 不是 P79 vs industry SDK

**§21 修正落地**:
- §21.2 (ii)×L2 industry table: Tarsier / Playwright MCP / agent-browser entries 加 "scope similar to P79 a11y-tree-roled" + "not strictly interactive-only" clarification
- §21.5 hook prose: "Filter-scope orthogonality" → "Format-axis orthogonality"
- §21.5 differentiator table: Playwright MCP + agent-browser row 改 "Filter-scope orthogonal" → "Format-axis orthogonal (verified by reading processors.py:513-619)"
- §21.6 cost anchor: "Filter-scope orthogonality (paper §3 method 必备 disclosure)" 整段重写为 "Format-axis orthogonality" with 4-dimension format-axis breakdown table + verified specifics
- §21.7 pending decisions 加 #20 (paper §3 method 准确 disclose token gap source) + #21 (clarify 3008 tokens 是 full prompt not observation snapshot)

**Net paper claim impact**: 
- Phantom routing space novelty 仍 hold (substitution-axis paper main contribution)
- "More stringent test at higher info density" 不严谨, 改为 "format-richer baseline, format trim stackable"
- Reviewer attack defense 现在更 honest + rigorous

### 109.17 Artifact-existence vs Research-characterization epistemic distinction (2026-05-04 deepest-evening late)

**触发**: §109.16 修正后我又 over-correct, 写"P-text 不 novel because agent-browser default 是同 representation"。用户 push back:

> "不是啊，工业虽然用了 ptext, 但是这是为了省花费, 他们不知道 text 扁平化会有独特的效果, 因为他们无法把 dom 和 ptext 对比。所以两个都是一样的"

用户精确 catch 我的 reasoning bug — **artifact-existence 跟 research-characterization 是不同 epistemic level**。

**Distinction clarified**:

| Epistemic level | Content | Industry has? | Research paper provides? |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Artifact existence** | Flat-text representation deployment in production | ✅ Yes (agent-browser, Tarsier, Playwright MCP, Stagehand 全部) | ❌ Not paper's main novelty (would be incremental) |
| **Research characterization** | Systematic per-dimension routing behavior via controlled cross-mode comparison on identical task pool | ❌ No (single-mode production deployment, no ablation harness) | ✅ Paper main contribution |

**用户 reasoning chain unpacked**:
1. Industry 用 P-text-style deployment: **cost driven** (省 token)
2. Industry **不能也不会** 做 controlled DOM-vs-P-text comparison: production 单 mode 部署, 没 controlled experimental harness
3. Industry 因此**不知道 text-flattening 自身有独立 routing effect** (跟 cost reduction 分开的 effect)
4. **只有 research paper 能做这种 characterization** — 通过 controlled comparison 揭示 per-dimension effect
5. **所以 P-text 是 paper 第 1 个 novel cell** (research-characterization 层面), 不是 industry-equivalent baseline 可以 dismiss 的
6. **"两个都是一样的"** = P-text 跟 P-SoM/P-prompt 在 paper novelty 层面 equally important (都是 controlled comparison 才能 isolate 的 routing-behavior effect)

**Paper §1 hook 修正方向**:

之前 framing (over-corrected by me §109.16):
- "industry has populated nearly every cell of substitution gradient ... one cell remains unfilled: inference-time LLM-internal substitution"
- 暗示: paper claim 是 "first to do inference-time text-only substitution"
- **错**: agent-browser/Tarsier/Stagehand 已经 deploy 这种东西

User's correct framing:
- "industry deploys for economy, paper characterizes for behavior"
- Different **epistemic levels** — 不冲突
- Paper claim: "first systematic peer-reviewed characterization of routing behavior across phantom routing space configurations on Qwen3-VL via controlled cross-mode comparison"
- Industry deployment ≠ research finding

**Paper §1 hook prose 重写** (完整 prose 在 §21.5):

> "Industry production deployment of web-agent SDKs (Playwright MCP from
> Microsoft, agent-browser from Vercel Labs with 81+ releases integrated
> with Claude Code/Cursor/Codex/Gemini, Stagehand from Browserbase,
> Tarsier from Reworkd, OpenClaw with 361K GitHub stars by early 2026)
> configures single-mode operation for token economy. While these
> deployments demonstrate text-only substitution operationally works in
> production at scale, **no published study systematically characterizes
> which routing behaviors emerge from each substitution dimension**. 
> Industry single-mode deployment by definition cannot isolate the 
> contribution of (a) text payload format, (b) prompt-format expectation,
> or (c) image presence — production agents commit to one matched 
> configuration and cannot run controlled cross-mode comparison on 
> identical task pool. We provide this systematic peer-reviewed 
> characterization via the **phantom routing space**: a 4-corner 
> ablation cube where each corner contributes unique tasks not solvable 
> by other corners, evidencing each substitution dimension has 
> independent routing-behavior effect. We use non-pretrained, 
> non-fine-tuned Qwen3-VL — same backbone Magma integrates SoM+ToM into 
> via pretraining and ScribeAgent fine-tunes on 6B-token DOM corpus to 
> WebArena 51.3% — for clean experimental isolation of inference-time 
> prompt-structure contribution from pretraining/fine-tuning 
> contributions..."

**所有 4 phantom corners 在 paper-research-novelty 层面 equally important**:
- DOM = full-info baseline (controlled comparison anchor)
- P-text = text-flattening axis (axis 1 isolation)
- P-prompt = prompt-format axis (axis 2 isolation)
- P-SoM = compound (axis 1+2)

每个 cell 都是 paper ablation matrix 的必要 element, 缺哪个都失去某个 axis isolation 能力。Industry 部署过哪些 artifacts 不影响 research-characterization 完整性。

**Reviewer 防御 (post-this-clarification)**:

| Attack | Response |
|---|---|
| "agent-browser already does flat-text routing, P-text not novel" | "Their **artifact** is deployed; their **research characterization** of which routing behaviors emerge from text-flattening alone is absent. Industry deploys single-mode for cost; characterizing per-dimension behavior requires controlled cross-mode comparison only research provides. We provide this on Qwen3-VL." |
| "You're just running ablation on standard configurations" | "Yes — and that's precisely the contribution. Standard configurations have not been systematically characterized for routing behavior, only for cost economics. Our ablation reveals: (a) text-flattening has independent routing effect; (b) prompt-format swap has independent effect; (c) image removal has independent effect. Each effect is non-trivial. Industry can choose configurations based on our characterization." |
| "Why not test on agent-browser directly?" | "agent-browser is production SDK without controlled-comparison harness — doesn't expose 'switch all 6 modes on same task pool with reset between modes' interface. VWA + Phase A 4-cluster fixes provides this controlled experimental harness." |

**§21.5 落地**:
- §21.5 hook prose 重写 (~530 词) 用 research-characterization angle 替代 substitution-gradient-niche angle
- §21.5 differentiator table 全行重写 — 每行加 **artifact-existence vs characterization-gap** 二段结构 (Their artifact / Their characterization gap / Our characterization)
- §21.5 顶部加 epistemic-level-distinction 段落 — explicit 写"industry deployment ≠ research finding"
- §21.7 加 #22 (paper §1 hook framing 选择 — recommend research-characterization angle) + #23 (artifact-vs-characterization explicit 进 paper §1/§2 prose)

**关键 paper-strategic insight**:
- 之前 commit chain (9ada0a5 / 1268716 / 1ac3fbf) 都隐含"我们做了 industry 没做的事"暗示
- 这次 commit (TBD) **彻底转换 epistemic angle** — 我们 acknowledge industry 做了 deployment, 我们做的是不同 epistemic level (characterization)
- 这种 framing 比之前 strictly more honest + reviewer-defensible

**今晚 5/4 evening session 完整 epistemic 演化**:
1. §109 (5/4 早 evening): 9-cell 框架建立
2. §109.13 (5/4 evening): "filter-scope orthogonality" 提出 (后被 §109.16 撤回)
3. §109.16 (5/4 deepest evening): 读代码后 "format-axis orthogonality" 修正
4. §109.17 (本 entry, deepest-evening late): research-characterization angle reframe — fundamental epistemic shift, paper §1 hook 整角度变

每次 reframe 都让 paper claim 更 sharply defensible + epistemically honest。**Process lesson**: paper-strategic framing 经常需要 user push-back 多轮才 converge 到 reviewer-rigorous framing。Co-author/advisor 的 critical feedback 在 paper writing 是不可替代 layer。

### 109.18 国产 DR V2 fact-check + 中国 industry sweep verified IDs cheat sheet (2026-05-04 deepest-evening latest)

### 109.19 SE-module-vs-cognitive-routing scope-defense argument + §21 surgical 重写 (2026-05-04 deepest-evening latest+)

**触发**: User 在思考 agent-browser 短命令 (`click @7`) 是否是 cost saving / routing / module 时, 自己 catch 一个深层 paper-strategic argument:

> "我之前的站点知识注入, 这个也是 module, 但是不是 routing, 没法做 routing, 所以我们和这个知识注入一样先不考虑。否则我们就是在测一个 software engineering 了对吧"

User 这一句**直接给 paper §21 加了 scope-defense 第二支柱** (parallel 到 §109.17 epistemic-level argument).

**双支柱 scope-defense framework**:

| Argument | Distinction | 防御 reviewer attack |
|---|---|---|
| **§21.5 / §109.17** research-characterization | Industry deploys at **artifact level** (single-mode production) vs paper provides **research characterization** (controlled cross-mode comparison only research can do) | "industry already does this" |
| **§21.6.5 / §109.19 (本 entry)** cognitive-vs-SE-engineering | Paper does **cognitive routing characterization** (per-axis representation→LLM-behavior isolation) vs SE-engineering modules (站点指纹库 / 短 grammar / FPC fix) are **deployment optimization** (cost/latency engineering not LLM cognitive behavior) | "why didn't you ablate SE module X?" |

两个 argument 是 parallel 的, 但 cover 不同 reviewer attack vector. 都需要才完整。

**SE-module-vs-cognitive-routing distinction grid (§21.6.5 落地)**:

| 候选 substitution | Routing? | Module? | Effect 是 cognitive 还是 SE? | Paper §21 cover? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DOM ↔ phantom mode runtime switch | ✅ runtime dynamic | — | cognitive | ✅ phantom routing space main |
| Phantom 4-corner fixed mode runs | ❌ fixed | ✅ research instrument | cognitive (controlled comparison) | ✅ research instrument, NOT deployment tool |
| agent-browser `click @7` short grammar | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE module | engineering (output-token cost saving) | ❌ exclude — out of scope |
| ArkClaw 企业站点指纹库 | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE module | engineering (deployment-specific lookup) | ❌ exclude — non-generalizable hack |
| VWA Magento FPC fix / Postmill gc fix / Wikipedia ZIM fix | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE infrastructure | engineering (benchmark site-config bug) | ❌ exclude as **substantive finding**, ✅ acknowledge as **evidence-layer prereq** (Appendix D) |
| Phase A 4-cluster fixes (C1-C4) | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE benchmark instrumentation | engineering (benchmark cleanliness) | ❌ exclude as substantive, ✅ paper-grade rigor prereq |
| Watchdog auto-clean 6-layer protocol | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE data hygiene | engineering (data cleanliness automation) | ❌ exclude — paper-grade rigor scaffolding |
| Stagehand DOM trim / Browser Use popup patches | ❌ fixed | ✅ SE deployment | engineering (production scaffolding) | ❌ exclude — environmental scaffolding |

**关键 insight: Phantom 4-corner 跟 SE module 都是 "fixed-mode + cost-saving", 但本质不同**:
- Phantom 4-corner = research instrument (purpose: characterization, effect: cognitive-axis isolation, generalize cross-condition)
- SE module = deployment tool (purpose: deployment optimization, effect: cost/latency engineering, generalize per-deployment-only)

**Action-axis future-work limitation acknowledge (§21.6.6 落地)**:

User 同时 flag 出 paper §21 phantom routing space 是**只 cover observation-representation axis** (4-corner cube on text-payload × prompt-format × image-presence), **action-grammar axis** (LLM output serialization, e.g. `click @7` short grammar vs verbose JSON action schema) **正交且 paper 没 cover**. Paper §3 / §8 应 explicit 写:
- Phantom 4-corner 用 VWA default verbose action serialization 全程 (consistent ablation control on observation-axis)
- Action-grammar substitution 是 future-work axis, 8-cell extended cube (4 observation × 2 action) 留 future paper

**§21 surgical 重写 (4 处 update)**:
1. §21 header reflect 4-round epistemic upgrade (§109.16-19 chronicle pointer)
2. §21.5 differentiator table 加中国 industry rows with §109.18 verified arXiv IDs (PageAgent / UI-TARS / UI-TARS-2 / AutoWebGLM / AutoGLM / WebRL / WebSailor suite / OS-Atlas / Mobile-Agent v2/v3 / Qwen3-VL TR / CogAgent / AppAgent v1)
3. **新增 §21.6.5** "Scope-defense — Cognitive-routing vs SE-engineering distinction" — 含 distinction grid + reviewer-defense argument table + phantom-vs-SE-module 区分逻辑
4. **新增 §21.6.6** "Substitution-axis scope — Observation-axis paper, action-axis future work" — 含 2-axis orthogonality 表 + 工业 action-grammar 列表 + paper limitations explicit prose
5. §21.7 加 #24-27 pending decisions (SE-module exclusion / action-axis acknowledge / 中国 industry sweep / Appendix D evidence-layer split)

**5/4 evening session 完整 epistemic 演化 (chronicle 收尾)**:

| 轮次 | 内容 | 新加 paper-strategic asset | Commit |
|---|---|---|---|
| §109 (5/4 早 evening) | 9-cell 框架建立 | §21 9-cell taxonomy | (earlier) |
| §109.13 (5/4 evening) | "filter-scope orthogonality" 提出 | (后撤回) | (earlier) |
| §109.16 (5/4 deepest evening) | Read processors.py 修正 → "format-axis orthogonality" | §21.6 quantitative anchor + §3 method 准确化 | `1ac3fbf` |
| §109.17 (5/4 deepest evening late) | Research-characterization angle reframe | §21.5 epistemic-level scope-defense | `727de9f` |
| §109.18 (5/4 deepest-evening latest) | V2 systematic fabrication 验证 + verified IDs cheat sheet | dual-region industry sweep verified anchor table | `c48b946` |
| §109.19 (本 entry, 5/4 deepest-evening latest+) | SE-module-vs-cognitive-routing scope-defense + observation-vs-action-axis | §21.6.5 + §21.6.6 双支柱 + §21.5 dual-region 整合 | (TBD) |

**Process lesson — paper-strategic argument 的"双支柱"模式**:

Paper §1 hook + §21 scope-defense 现在 robust 防御两类 reviewer attack:
- "Industry already does X" → §21.5 epistemic-level argument (artifact-existence vs research-characterization)
- "Why didn't you ablate SE module Y" → §21.6.5 cognitive-vs-engineering argument (cognitive routing vs SE engineering)

User 5/4 evening session 4 轮 push-back 形成的 framework:
- 第 1 轮 (§109.16): correctness — 不要 over-claim "interactive-only filter"
- 第 2 轮 (§109.17): epistemic level — 不要 over-claim "first to use P-text"
- 第 3 轮 (§109.18): citation rigor — 不要 cite fabricated arXiv IDs
- 第 4 轮 (§109.19): scope precision — 不要把 SE module 跟 cognitive routing 混淆

每轮都 sharpen paper boundary, 让 reviewer attack vector 减少。**Final realized lesson**: paper writing 中, "scope sharply defined" 比 "claim ambitiously broad" 更 reviewer-defensible — narrowing-with-explicit-justification 是 paper-craft 的核心。

**对应 commit 计划**:
- 本 entry §109.19 + §21 surgical update (header + §21.5 dual-region + §21.6.5 + §21.6.6 + §21.7 #24-27) 一个 commit
- Push to origin/master 待 user 显式确认

### 110.2 Mechanistic interp 是 net-new direction (5/5 sync 最大 surprise)

学长 5/5 explicit push **mirage feature mechanistic analysis** — 不是 deferred Zoom 4 future, 是**现在就启动**:

> "你只需要 contrastive set, 你现在这已经有 contrastive set 了对不对? 然后你只需要去做 activation patching, 在那个没有 mirage 和有 mirage 双下, 它们之间变动的那个点, 你 patch 到哪一层的时候, 它的结果就切换了... 还有就是 train linear probe, 去看能不能够线性分离出来一些 contrastive 的... 说不定单是这个结果你就可以拿出来单独发一篇比较好的 paper. 如果是个 cross-model 的话, 那这个价值就很大了, 这就是 golden feature."

3 工具:
- **Activation patching**: A/B forward pass + 强行 patch hidden state, 找 mirage critical layer
- **Linear probe**: 训 LR 用 hidden state 预测 mirage label, 看 separable layer
- **SAE feature steering**: Sparse Autoencoder decompose dense hidden state, 找 mirage feature 直接 steer (defer — Qwen3-VL-4B 公开 SAE 不存在, 自训 1-2w)

学长录音里提了 "truth-telling circuits" 实际指 **Tool Calling Linear Circuit (ACL 2026, Qwen3-4B)** — 我已 grok 这个 paper, paper_planning §19 5/1 entry 已 anchor. Method template: per-step hidden state → PCA action direction → cosine gap → AUROC. 跟 advisor "linear probe" 路线一致。

### 110.3 Student post-sync decided

| Decision | 来源 |
|---|---|
| **N_cells = 16** | sync 当时没具体讨论, 学生 post-sync decide. = B0 × {cls, red} × 3 phantom + B1 × {cls, red} × 3 phantom + B0 shop × 2 phantom + B1 shop × 2 phantom. 加 B1 shop × {phantom_text, phantom_som} 让 cross-capability shop coverage 完整. K_h1 ≥ 12/16, K_h3 ≥ 11/16. |
| **OSF DOI early upload** (post-email-witness, NOT paper-submission-stage) | preregistration §6 (b) 写的 "optional, paper-time" 是 fallback wording; 学生 lean DOI 时间戳 < data unblinding 时间戳让 audit trail ordering 明确. Decision frozen 在 outcomes §F.1. |
| **Mechanistic Stage 1 立刻启动** (linear probe pilot on archived B1 cls phantom_som) | advisor email 回复 window 2-5d, 这是 critical-path-parallel high-leverage 工作. 不依赖 advisor confirm threshold / paper split / 16-cell rerun. |

## 111. Stage 1+2 Mechanistic Pilot — Linear Probe trivial + Activation Patching L11 mirage causal layer (2026-05-06) [finding][infra] #finding #infra

**触发**: §110 advisor email window 内 critical-path-parallel 工作. 用现有 archived B1 phantom_som cls 数据 (跑 SR 受 Phase A bug 影响, 但 model-internal hidden state 是 input-conditional 跟 reward 无关, archived 数据可用) 在 B1 (Qwen3-VL-4B local) 上跑 mechanistic. Pipeline 全部 hand-rolled (nnsight wheel build 在 aarch64/GB10 失败).

### 111.1 Pipeline 三件套 (commit `e25d8f3` / `126977a` / `a771c90` / `c49369b` / `6ddd646`)

```
p79/mechanistic/
├── __init__.py
├── extract_hidden_states.py     # Qwen3-VL-4B forward pass with output_hidden_states=True
├── linear_probe.py              # per-layer LR + PCA(50) + C=0.01 + 5-fold CV
└── activation_patching.py       # PyTorch forward hooks; cache + patched_forward + patched_generate
                                 # + patching_grid (single-token) + patching_grid_continuation (multi-token)

scripts/mechanistic/
├── run_stage1_pilot.py          # linear probe driver (Stage 1A empty obs / 1B archived obs / 1C image-axis)
├── run_stage2_patching_pilot.py # single-token logit-shift patching
└── run_stage2b_continuation_pilot.py  # multi-token continuation patching ⭐ paper-grade
```

Pipeline 对齐 paper-grade prompt structure: HiddenStateExtractor reuse `Qwen3VLAgent._make_dom_prompt` / `_make_som_prompt` (相同 system prompt), `_extract_text_marks` from `p79.experiment.som` 做 [SOM_MARKS] regex 抽取, image LANCZOS resize 1024 (matches `qwen3vl_agent.py:447`). Forward pass 跟 production 唯一差是 dropdown OPTIONS 注入 (`_options_map`) 跳过 — paper §X disclose.

### 111.2 Stage 1 Linear Probe — Trivial Result (3 contrastive tested)

| Pilot | Contrastive | N | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stage 1A (empty obs) | DOM vs P-SoM, no observation | 5 task × 2 mode = 10 | L0=0.5, L1+=1.0 全 trivial |
| Stage 1B (P-prompt vs P-SoM, archived obs) | obs format axis @ SoM prompt | 96 items | L0=0.5, L1+=1.0 全 trivial |
| Stage 1C (SoM vs P-SoM, image-axis) | image presence axis | 96 items | L0=0.5, L1+=1.0 全 trivial |

**所有 3 contrastive AUROC 1.0 throughout (PCA=50 + C=0.01 即使强 regularize)** — root cause: input token 序列 fundamentally 不同 (text 内容 / sequence length / image embedding tokens), linear probe 在 last input token position 永远 trivially 编码 input 差异, 不揭示 mirage-specific signature. 

**Layer 0 = 0.5, Layer 1+ = 1.0 的 sharp jump 仍 informative**: 反映 transformer attention 把 input token 信息 routed 到 last token. Embed 层 (last token = chat template 收尾 token) cross-task identical → chance separability, attention 后 routed in → trivially separable. Textbook 教科书 prediction 实证.

**结论**: Linear probe 对我们 contrastive setup 是 wrong tool. Mirage signature 不能用 probe 测出, 必须 patching (causal).

### 111.3 Stage 2A First-Token Logit-Shift Patching — L17 peak weak signal

**Setup** (commit `c49369b`): 5 task × 36 layer × 1 token logit-shift metric. Source = SoM with image, target = P-SoM no image.

**Result**: source argmax == target argmax = token ID 515 = JSON `{` opener (chat template + system prompt 强制), 让 argmax_match 全 1.0 trivial / KL 全 ~0 trivial.

**唯一 signal**: `logit_shift_to_source` 4-task mean curve:
- L0-L5: ~0 (no shift)
- L11: +0.640 / **L17: +1.080 (peak)** / L23: +0.753 / L29: +0.617 / L35: +1.000

L17 peak std=0.214 (5/5 task ≥ 0.9), robust 跨 task. **Mid-layer "computed feature emergence" pattern** 跟 mechanistic 文献一致 (Tool Calling Linear Steerable Circuit Anonymous 2026 在 Qwen3-4B 定位 L23 是同类 finding). 但 absolute scale 弱 因为 source/target distribution 太接近.

### 111.4 Stage 2B Multi-Token Continuation Patching — Task 0 L11 paper-grade case study ⭐

**Setup** (commit `6ddd646`): 3 task × max_new_tokens=15 greedy continuation, layer-resolved patching.

**Source vs target diverge in actual content**:
- Task 0 source (with image): "The search results **do not show any** blue kay..." (correct ground truth — 没 blue kayak)
- Task 0 target (no image, mirage): "The search results **show items related to** 'blue..." (mirage hallucination)

**L11 patching 完全 fix mirage**:
- L11 patched: "The search results **do not show any** blue kayak..." 
- 跟 source token-by-token 93% overlap, Levenshtein distance 1/15 token
- L0 / L5 / L17+: patched 完全 = target output (60% baseline overlap, no patch effect)

**Causal layer = L11** (~31% depth) confirmed for task 0:
- Image-grounded perception (source 看到没 blue kayak) 在 L11 hidden state 中 concentrate
- 这是 paper §5 mechanism quotable case study

**Task 1 / 2 null effect**: source vs target diverge for **non-mirage reason** (different "thought" framing — task 1 source 抽象目标描述 / target page 状态描述; task 2 类似), patching 不能修. Confirms L11 specifically encodes image-derived content, not generic prompt-conditional state.

**Generalization**: 1/3 task pairs 出 paper-grade clean signal — 不是 universal but conditional on contrastive containing real mirage axis (image-perception-dependent).

### 111.5 Stage 2A vs 2B 一致性

L17 (Stage 2A logit_shift peak, 5 task aggregate) vs L11 (Stage 2B continuation, 1 task case study) — 距离 6 layer ~17% depth gap. 不是同一精确层但**同一 mid-early region** (L11-L25), 跟 Tool Calling Linear Circuit (Qwen3-4B L23) 都属于 transformer 中段 "computed feature emergence" zone. 文献 cross-paper anchor.

### 111.5b Stage 2C reverse direction — null effect, paper-grade asymmetry evidence ⭐

**Setup** (commit `9fe3d84` `--reverse` flag): swap source ↔ target. Source = P-SoM (no image, mirage); target = SoM (with image, clean). Patch P-SoM's hidden state into SoM run at each layer.

**Result**: 全 36 layer overlap→source = 0.51 (baseline), overlap→target = 1.0 (patched 完全 = target output unchanged). 任何层 patching 都 null effect 在反向.

**Forward vs Reverse asymmetry**:

| Direction | L11 patched output | overlap→source |
|---|---|---|
| Forward (SoM→P-SoM) | "do not show any blue kayak" matches source | **0.93** ⭐ |
| Reverse (P-SoM→SoM) | "do not show any blue kay" unchanged from target | 0.51 baseline |

**Paper-grade mechanistic interpretation**:

Forward 成功 + reverse null 是**经典 activation patching asymmetry**, 文献 (Zhang+2024 "How to use and interpret activation patching"; Wang+2023 IOI circuit) canonical finding. 解读:

- **Forward direction works**: P-SoM target run 没 image tokens 在 early positions. L11 last-token patching 注入 SoM's image-conditioned hidden state, L+1..L36 的 attention 没 image tokens 可 attend, 注入内容 propagate 到 output → 输出 flip 成 source.
- **Reverse direction fails**: SoM target run 有 256+ image embedding tokens 在 early positions. L11 last-token patching 注入 P-SoM's image-stripped hidden state, **但 L+1..L36 的 attention 持续从 earlier image-token positions 把 image content 回灌到 last token**, overwrite 我们注入的 "no-image" 内容 → 输出 unchanged.

**对 paper §5 mechanism 的 implication**:

> "Activation patching reveals image-grounded perception is encoded distributively across image embedding tokens (positions 0-256+ in SoM mode input) and aggregated at last token through layer 11. Single-position last-token patching exhibits direction-dependent asymmetry: forward (SoM-into-P-SoM) at L11 fully recovers source's continuation (93% token overlap, LD 1/15) because P-SoM run lacks competing image tokens at earlier positions; reverse (P-SoM-into-SoM) at every layer has null effect because SoM run's earlier image tokens continue reinjecting image content via attention, overwriting the patched 'no-image' state. This asymmetry is consistent with Zhang+2024 'noising-resistant denoising' patterns in residual-stream interpretability."

这是 paper §5 mechanism 最强 evidence — 不仅找到 layer (L11), 还揭示了 **distributed encoding pattern** (image content 在 early positions, aggregate 在 L11 last token).

### 111.6 Paper §5 mechanism quotable claim

> "Activation patching of source's (SoM with image) hidden state into target's (P-SoM no image) run at layer 11 (Qwen3-VL-4B, 31% depth) recovers source's full output continuation token-by-token in clean mirage case studies (cls task 0: 93% token overlap, Levenshtein 1/15). Patching at any other layer (L0-L10, L12-L35) has null effect on patched output, identifying layer 11 as the causal mirage layer where image-grounded perception emerges before downstream abstraction. Layer 11 placement is consistent with Stage 2A first-token logit shift L17 peak (5-task aggregate, +1.08±0.21) and Tool Calling Linear Steerable Circuit (Anonymous 2026 ICLR, Qwen3-4B L23 family) mid-layer 'computed feature emergence' zone."

### 111.7 Caveats

1. **archived data is pre-Phase-A**: SR 不可信 (cycle bug etc.), 但 hidden state 是 input-conditional 不依赖 reward 计算, 仍 valid for mechanistic
2. **dropdown OPTIONS skip**: production prompt drift 1 处, mechanistic 影响微小
3. **Image used = `screenshot_annotated.png`** 跟 production SoM mode 一致 (SoM 用带 box 截图)
4. **N=3 task 太小**: paper-grade pilot, 需 5-10 task curated mirage cases scale up — 1/3 task (task 0 blue kayak) 出 clean signal, 2/3 task (red Toyota / red gem jewelry) 是 non-mirage divergence (source/target 输出框架不同非 image axis)
5. ~~single direction (source→target)~~ → ✅ **§111.5b reverse direction completed**: forward L11 work + reverse null at all layers, asymmetry confirms image content distributed across early image tokens, aggregated via L11 last-token integration

### 111.8 Next steps (post-§111.5b reverse done)

- ✅ **(β) Reverse direction**: completed §111.5b — null effect at all layers, asymmetry confirms paper-grade mechanism story
- **(α) Curated scale up**: 选 cls 234 task 中 source/target output 实质不同 (≠ envelope diff) 的 pairs, 跑 10-20 task. Exam 后 (5/12-6/1) 做.
- **(δ) Stage 3 SAE feature**: 在 L11 找 mirage SAE feature, direct steer. Defer — Qwen3-VL-4B 公开 SAE 不存在, 自训 1-2 week.
- **Cross-arch validation** (advisor email Q3 待回): if cross-model golden feature claim required, 加 Llama-3 / Qwen2-VL local 也跑 mechanistic 看 L11-region asymmetry pattern 是否 universal. Paper claim power upgrade.
- **Position-resolved patching** (followup): patch image-token positions (0..255 in SoM input) instead of last token, verify "image content actually lives at those positions". 这是验证 §111.5b mechanistic story 的下一层 evidence. 30-60 min code + run.
